= True\n            prev_code_ch = \"\"\n            continue\n        prev_code_idx = i\n        prev_code_ch = ch\n    return \"\".join(out)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Line-state scanners (formerly _smell_helpers_line_state.py)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef _scan_template_content(\n    line: str, start: int, brace_depth: int = 0\n) -> tuple[int, bool, int]:\n    \"\"\"Scan template literal content from *start* in *line*.\"\"\"\n    j = start\n    while j < len(line):\n        ch = line[j]\n        if ch == \"\\\\\" and j + 1 < len(line):\n            j += 2\n            continue\n        if ch == \"$\" and j + 1 < len(line) and line[j + 1] == \"{\":\n            brace_depth += 1\n            j += 2\n            continue\n        if ch == \"}\" and brace_depth > 0:\n            brace_depth -= 1\n            j += 1\n            continue\n        if ch == \"`\" and brace_depth == 0:\n            return (j + 1, True, brace_depth)\n        j += 1\n    return (j, False, brace_depth)\n\n\ndef _scan_code_line(line: str) -> tuple[bool, bool, int]:\n    \"\"\"Scan a normal code line for block comment or template literal start.\"\"\"\n    j = 0\n    in_str = None\n    while j < len(line):\n        ch = line[j]\n\n        if in_str and ch == \"\\\\\" and j + 1 < len(line):\n            j += 2\n            continue\n\n        if in_str:\n            if ch == in_str:\n                in_str = None\n            j += 1\n            continue\n\n        if ch == \"/\" and j + 1 < len(line) and line[j + 1] == \"/\":\n            break\n\n        if ch == \"/\" and j + 1 < len(line) and line[j + 1] == \"*\":\n            close = line.find(\"*/\", j + 2)\n            if close != -1:\n                j = close + 2\n                continue\n            return (True, False, 0)\n\n        if ch == \"`\":\n            end_pos, found_close, depth = _scan_template_content(line, j + 1)\n            if found_close:\n                j = end_pos\n                continue\n            return (False, True, depth)\n\n        if ch in (\"'\", '\"'):\n            in_str = ch\n            j += 1\n            continue\n\n        j += 1\n\n    return (False, False, 0)\n\n\ndef _build_ts_line_state(lines: list[str]) -> dict[int, str]:\n    \"\"\"Build a map of lines inside block comments or template literals.\"\"\"\n    state: dict[int, str] = {}\n    in_block_comment = False\n    in_template = False\n    template_brace_depth = 0\n\n    for i, line in enumerate(lines):\n        if in_block_comment:\n            state[i] = \"block_comment\"\n            if \"*/\" in line:\n                in_block_comment = False\n            continue\n\n        if in_template:\n            state[i] = \"template_literal\"\n            _, found_close, template_brace_depth = _scan_template_content(\n                line, 0, template_brace_depth\n            )\n            if found_close:\n                in_template = False\n            continue\n\n        in_block_comment, in_template, depth = _scan_code_line(line)\n        if in_template:\n            template_brace_depth = depth\n\n    return state\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"_FileContext\",\n    \"_build_ts_line_state\",\n    \"_code_text\",\n    \"_content_line_info\",\n    \"_extract_block_body\",\n    \"_find_block_end\",\n    \"_scan_code_line\",\n    \"_scan_template_content\",\n    \"_strip_ts_comments\",\n    \"_track_brace_body\",\n    \"_ts_match_is_in_string\",\n    \"scan_code\",\n]\n"
```json
}
```

```json
  "content": "\"\"\"Nesting-depth and callback-depth complexity metrics for tree-sitter languages.\n\nAlso hosts shared helpers (ComputeFn, _ensure_parser) used by sibling modules.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, Callable\n\nfrom . import PARSE_INIT_ERRORS\nfrom ._cache import _PARSE_CACHE\nfrom ._extractors import _get_parser\n\nif TYPE_CHECKING:\n    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import TreeSitterLangSpec\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nComputeFn = Callable[[str, list[str]], tuple[int, str] | None]\n\"\"\"Signature for complexity signal compute functions.\n\nEach factory returns a closure with this effective call shape:\n``(content, lines, *, _filepath=\"\") -> (count, label) | None``.\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _ensure_parser(\n    cache: dict[str, Any],\n    spec: TreeSitterLangSpec,\n    *,\n    with_query: bool = False,\n) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Lazily initialise parser (and optionally function query) into *cache*.\"\"\"\n    if \"parser\" in cache:\n        return True\n    try:\n        parser, lang = _get_parser(spec.grammar)\n        cache[\"parser\"] = parser\n        cache[\"language\"] = lang\n        if with_query:\n            from ._extractors import _make_query\n\n            cache[\"query\"] = _make_query(lang, spec.function_query)\n    except PARSE_INIT_ERRORS as exc:\n        logger.debug(\"tree-sitter init failed: %s\", exc)\n        return False\n    return True\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Nesting depth\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n_NESTING_NODE_TYPES = frozenset(\n    {\n        \"if_statement\",\n        \"if_expression\",\n        \"if_let_expression\",\n        \"else_clause\",\n        \"elif_clause\",\n        \"for_statement\",\n        \"for_expression\",\n        \"for_in_statement\",\n        \"while_statement\",\n        \"while_expression\",\n        \"do_statement\",\n        \"loop_expression\",\n        \"try_statement\",\n        \"try_expression\",\n        \"catch_clause\",\n        \"rescue\",\n        \"except_clause\",\n        \"switch_statement\",\n        \"switch_expression\",\n        \"match_expression\",\n        \"case_clause\",\n        \"match_arm\",\n        \"with_statement\",\n        \"with_clause\",\n        \"lambda_expression\",\n        \"closure_expression\",\n    }\n)\n\n\ndef compute_nesting_depth_ts(\n    filepath: str, spec: TreeSitterLangSpec, parser, language\n) -> int | None:\n    \"\"\"Compute max control-flow nesting depth via iterative AST walk.\"\"\"\n    del language\n    cached = _PARSE_CACHE.get_or_parse(filepath, parser, spec.grammar)\n    if cached is None:\n        return None\n    _source, tree = cached\n\n    max_depth = 0\n    stack: list[tuple[object, int]] = [(tree.root_node, 0)]\n    while stack:\n        node, depth = stack.pop()\n        if node.type in _NESTING_NODE_TYPES:\n            depth += 1\n            if depth > max_depth:\n                max_depth = depth\n        for index in range(node.child_count - 1, -1, -1):\n            stack.append((node.children[index], depth))\n    return max_depth\n\n\ndef make_nesting_depth_compute(spec: TreeSitterLangSpec) -> ComputeFn:\n    \"\"\"Build a complexity compute callback for max nesting depth.\"\"\"\n    _cached_parser: dict[str, Any] = {}\n\n    def compute(content: str, lines: list[str], *, _filepath: str = \"\") -> tuple[int, str] | None:\n        del content, lines\n        if not _filepath:\n            return None\n        if not _ensure_parser(_cached_parser, spec):\n            return None\n\n        depth = compute_nesting_depth_ts(\n            _filepath,\n            spec,\n            _cached_parser[\"parser\"],\n            _cached_parser[\"language\"],\n        )\n        if depth is None or depth <= 0:\n            return None\n        return depth, f\"nesting depth {depth}\"\n\n    return compute\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Callback / closure nesting depth\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n_CLOSURE_NODE_TYPES = frozenset(\n    {\n        \"arrow_function\",\n        \"function_expression\",\n        \"function\",\n        \"lambda_expression\",\n        \"closure_expression\",\n        \"lambda\",\n        \"anonymous_function\",\n        \"block_argument\",\n        \"func_literal\",\n    }\n)\n\n\ndef make_callback_depth_compute(spec: TreeSitterLangSpec) -> ComputeFn:\n    \"\"\"Build a complexity compute callback for callback/closure nesting depth.\"\"\"\n    _cached_parser: dict[str, Any] = {}\n\n    def compute(content: str, lines: list[str], *, _filepath: str = \"\") -> tuple[int, str] | None:\n        del content, lines\n        if not _filepath:\n            return None\n        if not _ensure_parser(_cached_parser, spec):\n            return None\n\n        parser = _cached_parser[\"parser\"]\n        cached = _PARSE_CACHE.get_or_parse(_filepath, parser, spec.grammar)\n        if cached is None:\n            return None\n        _source, tree = cached\n\n        max_depth = 0\n        stack: list[tuple[object, int]] = [(tree.root_node, 0)]\n        while stack:\n            node, depth = stack.pop()\n            if node.type in _CLOSURE_NODE_TYPES:\n                depth += 1\n                if depth > max_depth:\n                    max_depth = depth\n            for index in range(node.child_count - 1, -1, -1):\n                stack.append((node.children[index], depth))\n\n        if max_depth <= 1:\n            return None\n        return max_depth, f\"callback depth {max_depth}\"\n\n    return compute\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/treesitter/__init__.py",
  "old_string": "def get_spec(language: str) -> TreeSitterLangSpec | None:\n    \"\"\"Return tree-sitter spec for a language key, if configured.\"\"\"\n    key = str(language or \"\").strip().lower()\n    if not key:\n        return None\n    from ._specs import TREESITTER_SPECS\n\n    return TREESITTER_SPECS.get(key)\n\n\ndef list_specs() -> dict[str, TreeSitterLangSpec]:\n    \"\"\"Return a shallow copy of the public tree-sitter spec registry.\"\"\"\n    from ._specs import TREESITTER_SPECS\n\n    return dict(TREESITTER_SPECS)",
  "new_string": "def _build_treesitter_specs() -> dict[str, TreeSitterLangSpec]:\n    \"\"\"Build the TREESITTER_SPECS dict from the three spec modules.\"\"\"\n    from . import _specs_compiled as _sc, _specs_functional as _sf, _specs_scripting as _ss\n\n    return {\n        \"go\": _sc.GO_SPEC, \"rust\": _sc.RUST_SPEC, \"java\": _sc.JAVA_SPEC,\n        \"kotlin\": _sc.KOTLIN_SPEC, \"csharp\": _sc.CSHARP_SPEC,\n        \"swift\": _sc.SWIFT_SPEC, \"php\": _sc.PHP_SPEC, \"dart\": _sc.DART_SPEC,\n        \"c\": _sc.C_SPEC, \"cpp\": _sc.CPP_SPEC, \"scala\": _sc.SCALA_SPEC,\n        \"elixir\": _sf.ELIXIR_SPEC, \"erlang\": _sf.ERLANG_SPEC,\n        \"fsharp\": _sf.FSHARP_SPEC, \"haskell\": _sf.HASKELL_SPEC,\n        \"ocaml\": _sf.OCAML_SPEC, \"clojure\": _sf.CLOJURE_SPEC,\n        \"ruby\": _ss.RUBY_SPEC, \"javascript\": _ss.JS_SPEC,\n        \"typescript\": _ss.TYPESCRIPT_SPEC, \"bash\": _ss.BASH_SPEC,\n        \"lua\": _ss.LUA_SPEC, \"perl\": _ss.PERL_SPEC, \"zig\": _ss.ZIG_SPEC,\n        \"nim\": _ss.NIM_SPEC, \"powershell\": _ss.POWERSHELL_SPEC,\n        \"gdscript\": _ss.GDSCRIPT_SPEC, \"r\": _ss.R_SPEC,\n    }\n\n\ndef get_spec(language: str) -> TreeSitterLangSpec | None:\n    \"\"\"Return tree-sitter spec for a language key, if configured.\"\"\"\n    key = str(language or \"\").strip().lower()\n    if not key:\n        return None\n    return _build_treesitter_specs().get(key)\n\n\ndef list_specs() -> dict[str, TreeSitterLangSpec]:\n    \"\"\"Return a shallow copy of the public tree-sitter spec registry.\"\"\"\n    return dict(_build_treesitter_specs())"
}
```
